## AI and Data Engineering

### Apache Spark

#### GPU Acceleration

  - **(2026)** [**How to Rewrite Apache Spark UDFs for GPU Acceleration with AI Agents**](https://developer.nvidia.com/blog/rewrite-apache-spark-udfs-gpu-acceleration-ai-agents) <span class='md-tag md-tag--warning'>[EN CONTENT]</span> 🌟🌟🌟🌟 <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[AI-AGENTS]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[APACHE-SPARK]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[DATA-ENGINEERING]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--warning'>[EMERGING]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--success'>[ENTERPRISE-STABLE]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[GPU]</span> — User-defined functions (UDFs) in Apache Spark break the declarative abstraction, forcing GPU-accelerated pipelines to fall back to CPU execution and incur data transfer overhead. This post explores rewriting UDFs using Spark SQL expressions, the RapidsUDF interface, or custom CUDA kernels via JNI to eliminate fallback and achieve over 20x speedups. Furthermore, developers can leverage agent-driven workflows with cuDF skills for Apache Spark to guide testing, conversion, benchmarking, and optimization.

#### Agent Security

  - **(2026)** [**NVIDIA Open Agent Safety Platform: A Reference for Continuous In-Silicon Agent Monitoring**](https://developer.nvidia.com/blog/nvidia-open-agent-safety-platform-a-reference-for-continuous-in-silicon-agent-monitoring) <span class='md-tag md-tag--warning'>[EN CONTENT]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--critical'>[ADVANCED LEVEL]</span> 🌟🌟🌟🌟 <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[AGENT]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[BLUEFIELD-4]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--warning'>[EMERGING]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--success'>[ENTERPRISE-STABLE]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[GPU]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[SECURITY]</span> — NVIDIA's Open Agent Safety Platform establishes a multi-layered security boundary specifically designed to govern autonomous AI agents. Curator insight reveals that it integrates the OpenShell software runtime, Sentry watchdog software, and BlueField-4 DPU hardware to enforce runtime policies off-host. Live grounding confirms that this infrastructure-level quarantine capability can intercept rogue agent behaviors in milliseconds, providing an enterprise-stable foundation for deploying high-risk autonomous workloads.

### Safety and Alignment

#### Content Moderation

- **Kubernetes & Local Execution**: Runs workloads natively on rootless Podman 5 locally and standard Kubernetes Jobs in production clusters without custom CRD operator sprawl.
- **Enterprise Control Plane**: Enforces tenant isolation via PostgreSQL Row-Level Security, streams execution telemetry via NATS JetStream, and exposes REST, Model Context Protocol (MCP), gitolite-style Git ACLs, and human-in-the-loop approval inboxes.

* **Curator Insight**: The architectural value lies in treating complex generative models as composable microservices, fundamentally accelerating developer velocity for AI applications.
* **Live Grounding**: The workflow leverages the `agents.md` schema exposed by Gradio Spaces, enabling a coding agent to autonomously chain text-to-image and 3D Gaussian splatting endpoints without manual software intervention.

- **Safety Architecture**: Enforces strict `kubectl` command classification tiers (run-freely, ask-first, never-run) verified in CI against an allow-list, prohibits reading Secrets, and attaches rollback commands to any proposed change.
- **Live Verification**: Each skill is tested against intentionally broken `kind` clusters using view-only `kubeconfig` credentials to validate deterministic root-cause isolation.
### Multi-repo Agent Architectures

  - **(2026)** [**The Home Repo: The effortless multi-repo agent setup**](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=hWe-j78vNpc) 🌟🌟🌟🌟 <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[AI]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--warning'>[EMERGING]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--success'>[ENTERPRISE-STABLE]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--secondary'>[GUIDE]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[MCP]</span> — Architectural walkthrough by Matt Pocock (AI Hero) presenting the "Home Repo" pattern for orchestrating AI coding agents across multiple decoupled Git repositories without forcing a disruptive monorepo migration. Rather than fragmenting agent context inside isolated repos, engineering teams maintain a dedicated orchestration repository housing cross-repo steering files, reusable Agent Skills, shared personal wikis, and automation tooling.
- **Multi-Repo Context Cohesion**: Bridges context boundaries across microservice, infrastructure-as-code, and frontend repositories so autonomous agents understand end-to-end dependencies.
- **Platform Engineering Applicability**: Directly adaptable to Kubernetes and GitOps estates where Helm charts, Terraform/OpenTofu modules, and application source trees live in separate repositories.

  - **(2026)** [**How Specializing the Vocabulary of SLMs Can Cut Inference Cost by 77% in Real Applications**](https://huggingface.co/blog/Dharma-AI/vocabx-vocabulary-specialization) <span class='md-tag md-tag--warning'>[EN CONTENT]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--critical'>[ADVANCED LEVEL]</span> 🌟🌟🌟🌟 <span class='md-tag md-tag--warning'>[EMERGING]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--success'>[ENTERPRISE-STABLE]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[FINETUNING]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--secondary'>[GUIDE]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[INFERENCE]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[MODEL-OPTIMIZATION]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[SLM]</span> — Dharma-AI details a technique for specializing the token vocabulary of Small Language Models (SLMs) tailored for targeted domains. By fine-tuning open-source foundations (like Qwen and Gemma) and simultaneously pruning their generalized vocabulary, the approach radically decreases memory footprint and cuts inference costs by up to 77%. This optimization strategy proves that aligning a model's training history and vocabulary directly with the deployment task yields superior production stability and efficiency compared to relying solely on commercial frontier APIs.

#### Foundation Models

  - **(2026)** [**Ensemble forecasting with IBM Granite time-series models**](https://huggingface.co/blog/ibm-research/granite-timeseries-ensemble-r1) <span class='md-tag md-tag--warning'>[EN CONTENT]</span> 🌟🌟🌟🌟 <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[APACHE-FLINK]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--secondary'>[CASE STUDY]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--warning'>[EMERGING]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--success'>[ENTERPRISE-STABLE]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[FOUNDATION-MODELS]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[STREAMING]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[TIME-SERIES]</span> — IBM Research explores ensemble forecasting techniques using the Granite Time Series foundation models, specifically highlighting the commercial-friendly PatchTST-FM-r2 release. ## Accelerated Computing

### Computer Vision (1)

#### Scientific Imaging

  - **(2026)** [**Faster Scientific Image Analysis with NVIDIA cuPhoton**](https://developer.nvidia.com/blog/faster-scientific-image-analysis-with-nvidia-cuphoton) <span class='md-tag md-tag--warning'>[EN CONTENT]</span> 🌟🌟🌟🌟 <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[COMPUTER-VISION]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[CUPHOTON]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--warning'>[EMERGING]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--success'>[ENTERPRISE-STABLE]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[GPU]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[SCIENTIFIC-COMPUTING]</span> — High-throughput scientific instruments such as observatories and X-ray light sources generate image data at rates that quickly overwhelm CPU-bound pipelines. NVIDIA cuPhoton is an open-source CUDA-X toolkit designed to move end-to-end scientific image analysis entirely onto NVIDIA GPUs. By eliminating CPU bottlenecks and enabling multi-GPU distributed workflows, researchers can accelerate the loading, alignment, and analysis of astronomical and photonics images at petabyte scale.
### Decision Optimization

#### Linear Programming

  - **(2026)** [**Scaling Decision Optimization to 100 Million Variables and Beyond with mPDLP in NVIDIA cuOpt**](https://developer.nvidia.com/blog/scaling-decision-optimization-to-100-million-variables-and-beyond-with-mpdlp-in-nvidia-cuopt) <span class='md-tag md-tag--warning'>[EN CONTENT]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--critical'>[ADVANCED LEVEL]</span> 🌟🌟🌟🌟 <span class='md-tag md-tag--warning'>[EMERGING]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--success'>[ENTERPRISE-STABLE]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[GPU]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[LINEAR-PROGRAMMING]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[OPTIMIZATION]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[SUPPLY-CHAIN]</span> — NVIDIA cuOpt has introduced the Multi-GPU Primal-Dual hybrid gradient for Linear Programming (mPDLP) solver to tackle massive optimization problems across SKUs, supply chain lanes, and energy grids. By distributing large linear programming workloads across NVLink-connected GPUs, mPDLP handles up to 100 million variables and 2.1 billion nonzeros while dramatically reducing peak memory usage. Utilizing min-cut graph partitioning to minimize cross-GPU communication, the solver achieves up to 11.4x speedups on large-scale instances, enabling unprecedented decision intelligence capabilities.

  - **(2026)** [**Thinking of ACE? We Can Do It with Fewer Tokens**](https://huggingface.co/blog/ibm-research/altk-evolve-sldd) <span class='md-tag md-tag--warning'>[EN CONTENT]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--critical'>[ADVANCED LEVEL]</span> 🌟🌟🌟🌟 <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[AI-AGENTS]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[COST-REDUCTION]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--warning'>[EMERGING]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--success'>[ENTERPRISE-STABLE]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[FINOPS]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[LLM-OPTIMIZATION]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[LLM]</span> — IBM Research presents ALTK-Evolve as an alternative to the ACE framework, designed to evolve an AI agent's working context using significantly fewer tokens. Instead of aggregating dense historical summaries, it extracts and retains compact guidelines and playbooks directly from trajectory iterations. *Curator Insight*: This represents a fundamental breakthrough in LLM FinOps; by preventing context window saturation, it enables highly sustained and cost-effective autonomous agent loops in production.

  - **(2026)** [**NVIDIA Nemotron 3.5 Super VL Powers Specialized Agents with Multimodal Reasoning**](https://developer.nvidia.com/blog/nvidia-nemotron-3-5-super-vl-powers-specialized-agents-with-multimodal-reasoning) <span class='md-tag md-tag--warning'>[EN CONTENT]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--critical'>[ADVANCED LEVEL]</span> 🌟🌟🌟🌟 <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[AI-AGENTS]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--success'>[DE FACTO STANDARD]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--success'>[ENTERPRISE-STABLE]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[GPU]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[LLM]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[MULTIMODAL]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[OPEN-WEIGHTS]</span> — NVIDIA Nemotron 3.5 Super VL is a 120-billion parameter (12B active) open-weight mixture-of-experts (MoE) model engineered specifically for long-running multimodal AI agents. Generating over 600 tokens per second, it provides enterprise teams with a highly efficient foundation for complex reasoning, tool manipulation, and autonomous workflows.
### Security and Governance

  - **(2026)** [**Inside NVIDIA Sentry: Secure AI Agents at Runtime**](https://developer.nvidia.com/blog/inside-nvidia-sentry-secure-ai-agents-at-runtime) <span class='md-tag md-tag--warning'>[EN CONTENT]</span> 🌟🌟🌟🌟 <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[AI-AGENTS]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[BLUEFIELD]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--warning'>[EMERGING]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--success'>[ENTERPRISE-STABLE]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[GPU]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[RUNTIME-PROTECTION]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[SECURITY]</span> — NVIDIA Sentry provides a runtime security framework designed to protect AI agents from crossing security boundaries during task execution. It governs agent access requests, monitors runtime activity, and contains threats directly from an isolated hardware security layer in milliseconds. Leveraging NVIDIA BlueField DPUs, this in-silicon enforcement quarantines rogue agentic behavior before infrastructure compromises occur, bringing zero-trust architectural principles to autonomous workloads.
  - **(2026)** [**How NVIDIA OpenShell Reference Architecture Secures and Governs AI Agent Workloads at Scale**](https://developer.nvidia.com/blog/how-nvidia-openshell-reference-architecture-secures-and-governs-ai-agent-workloads-at-scale) <span class='md-tag md-tag--warning'>[EN CONTENT]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--critical'>[ADVANCED LEVEL]</span> 🌟🌟🌟🌟 <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[AI-AGENTS]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[ARCHITECTURE]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--success'>[DE FACTO STANDARD]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--success'>[ENTERPRISE-STABLE]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[GPU]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[SANDBOXING]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[SECURITY]</span> — NVIDIA OpenShell is an open-source runtime reference architecture that enforces security and governance for agentic systems by treating agent workloads as inherently untrusted. The framework utilizes four key components—Gateway, Supervisor, Sandbox, and Policy Prover—to manage sandbox lifecycles, mediate credential access, isolate workloads, and formally verify policy permissions. Optimized for NVIDIA Vera CPUs, it enables hardware-isolated monitoring that can support thousands of concurrent, securely contained agent environments within an enterprise AI factory.

### Machine Learning

#### System One Models

  - **(2026)** [**Introducing Clairic, a new System One class of extraction models**](https://blog.cloudflare.com/clairic-extraction-model) <span class='md-tag md-tag--warning'>[EN CONTENT]</span> 🌟🌟🌟🌟 <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[AI]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[EDGE-COMPUTING]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--warning'>[EMERGING]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--success'>[ENTERPRISE-STABLE]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[LLM]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[MACHINE-LEARNING]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[SYSTEM-ONE]</span> — Cloudflare has launched Clairic, the foundational model in a novel 'System One' class tailored specifically for data extraction, computation, and estimation tasks. Unlike traditional LLMs that rely on auto-regressive generation, Clairic computes outputs in a single forward pass, eliminating output token costs and drastically reducing latency. It accepts unstructured text and up to four images alongside a strongly-typed schema to produce deterministic, structured data outputs. This architecture provides an extremely fast, economical, and accurate alternative for clerical automation and data regression tasks at the edge.

#### Mixture-of-experts

  - **(2026)** [**Dense vs. MoE Models: Active Parameters, Throughput, and When to Choose Each**](https://developer.nvidia.com/blog/dense-vs-moe-models-active-parameters-throughput-and-when-to-choose-each) <span class='md-tag md-tag--warning'>[EN CONTENT]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--critical'>[ADVANCED LEVEL]</span> 🌟🌟🌟🌟 <span class='md-tag md-tag--warning'>[EMERGING]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--success'>[ENTERPRISE-STABLE]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[GPU]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--secondary'>[GUIDE]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[INFERENCE]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[LLM]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[MOE]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[NEMOTRON]</span> — This technical analysis contrasts dense neural networks with Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures, focusing on throughput, parameter activation, and deployment trade-offs. Live grounding confirms the evaluation of Nemotron 3.5 Lightning, which decouples memory cost from computational load by selectively activating only 3B out of 30B parameters per token. From a structural perspective, this approach yields significantly higher token throughput compared to dense equivalents at identical parameter scales, though latency advantages diminish under high concurrency. The guide provides essential architectural considerations for memory budgeting, quantization behavior, and routing strategy when fine-tuning and deploying large-scale inference systems.
#### Transformer Algorithms

  - **(2026)** [**DiScoFormer: One transformer for density and score, across distributions**](https://huggingface.co/blog/allenai/discoformer) <span class='md-tag md-tag--warning'>[EN CONTENT]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--critical'>[ADVANCED LEVEL]</span> 🌟🌟🌟🌟 <span class='md-tag md-tag--warning'>[EMERGING]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--success'>[ENTERPRISE-STABLE]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[GENERATIVE-AI]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[STATISTICAL-MODELING]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[TRANSFORMERS]</span> — DiScoFormer introduces a single Transformer model capable of estimating both the density and the score of any data distribution in a single forward pass, eliminating the need for per-problem retraining. By leveraging a shared backbone with dual output heads, it overcomes the dimensional limitations of Kernel Density Estimation (KDE) and the training overhead of traditional neural score-matching. This breakthrough significantly accelerates generative modeling and Bayesian inference workflows that depend on high-dimensional probability approximations.

  - **(2026)** [**Featuring Every Eval Ever Results on Hugging Face Model Pages**](https://huggingface.co/blog/eee-community-evals) <span class='md-tag md-tag--warning'>[EN CONTENT]</span> 🌟🌟🌟🌟 <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[BENCHMARKING]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--warning'>[EMERGING]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--success'>[ENTERPRISE-STABLE]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[EVALS]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[OPEN-SCIENCE]</span> — Hugging Face has integrated the Every Eval Ever (EEE) framework with Community Evals to decentralize and standardize benchmark reporting across the AI ecosystem. By encapsulating autotuned kernels, tensor parallelism, context prefix reuse, and MTP speculative decoding into a validated container, NIM delivers up to 2.5x greater user concurrency for agentic AI tasks. This approach transitions optimization from a manual engineering burden into a deployable, enterprise-ready artifact with standard APIs.
#### Inference Optimization (3)

  - **(2026)** [**When to Use Encode-Prefill-Decode Disaggregation to Accelerate Multimodal Model Serving**](https://developer.nvidia.com/blog/when-to-use-encode-prefill-decode-disaggregation-to-accelerate-multimodal-model-serving) <span class='md-tag md-tag--warning'>[EN CONTENT]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--critical'>[ADVANCED LEVEL]</span> 🌟🌟🌟🌟 <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[DISAGGREGATION]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--warning'>[EMERGING]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--success'>[ENTERPRISE-STABLE]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[GPU]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[INFERENCE]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[LLM]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[MULTIMODAL]</span> — This technical analysis presents Encode-Prefill-Decode (EPD) disaggregation as a vital optimization strategy for serving multimodal large language models using NVIDIA Dynamo. By physically separating the computationally expensive vision encoder stage from the LLM's prefill and decode phases, the architecture allows text-only requests to bypass visual processing entirely. Testing reveals up to 5x faster time-to-first-token by permitting independent batching and hardware scaling—such as using BF16 for encoders while deploying NVFP4 for decoding. The article provides definitive guidance on when to employ aggregated, co-located, or fully disaggregated topological setups based on prompt composition and model type.

NVIDIA Megatron Core implements mechanisms to enforce deterministic execution across massive clusters, ensuring that model training remains stable and verifiable even at immense scale. This approach significantly reduces the overhead associated with troubleshooting and checkpoint recovery during complex generative AI training runs.
#### Optimization Frameworks

It focuses on how the NVIDIA Transformer Engine resolves severe inter-GPU communication bottlenecks by introducing specialized kernels designed to process ragged tensors and uneven token distributions. Critical architectural optimizations include JAX host offloading, XLA multistreaming, and grouped GEMM via cuBLASLt. These enhancements effectively eliminate GPU idle times, making dropless MoE training highly efficient at production scale.

### Policy and Compliance

## Digital Biology

### Protein Design

  - **(2026)** [**Design Proteins with RFD4-Proteina Using Reward-Guided Inference-Time Search**](https://developer.nvidia.com/blog/design-proteins-with-rfd4-proteina-using-reward-guided-inference-time-search) <span class='md-tag md-tag--warning'>[EN CONTENT]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--critical'>[ADVANCED LEVEL]</span> 🌟🌟🌟🌟 <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[AI]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[BIONEMO]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[DIGITAL-BIOLOGY]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--warning'>[EMERGING]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--success'>[ENTERPRISE-STABLE]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[FOUNDATION-MODELS]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[GPU]</span> — RFD4-Proteina is a biological foundation model that co-designs protein sequences and all-atom structures using reward-guided inference-time search to focus compute on promising candidates. By combining explicit backbone coordinates with latent representation, it removes the separate inverse-folding handoff, enabling 3.8x faster generation and an 84% reduction in peak memory compared to prior approaches. The model integrates with CUDA-accelerated oracles like OpenFold3 to score and refine designs against geometry and interaction metrics, making it a powerful tool for accelerating end-to-end co-folding performance in drug discovery.

This declarative approach allows engineering teams to seamlessly integrate requirements management and thermal simulations to execute capacity planning studies without disrupting source assets.
## Distributed Inference (1)

### Multi-node Orchestration

  - **(2026)** [**Running Frontier-Class AI Models Across 8 NVIDIA DGX Spark Nodes**](https://developer.nvidia.com/blog/running-frontier-class-ai-models-across-8-nvidia-dgx-spark-nodes) <span class='md-tag md-tag--warning'>[EN CONTENT]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--critical'>[ADVANCED LEVEL]</span> 🌟🌟🌟🌟 <span class='md-tag md-tag--secondary'>[CASE STUDY]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[DGX-SPARK]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[DISTRIBUTED-INFERENCE]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--warning'>[EMERGING]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--success'>[ENTERPRISE-STABLE]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[GPU]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[LLM]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[MODEL-ORCHESTRATION]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[VLLM]</span> — Addressing the complexities of orchestrating massive, frontier-class AI models, this article outlines the architecture for deploying chained inference workloads across a cluster of 8 NVIDIA DGX Spark nodes. The engineers utilize the `spark-vllm-docker` project to facilitate distributed execution and dynamic model parallelism across interconnected systems. This multi-node approach enables developers to seamlessly run complex, multi-model agentic pipelines that exceed the VRAM capacity and compute boundaries of a single high-performance workstation.
## Edge Computing

### AI Inference

#### Security and Isolation

  - **(2026)** [**Cross-request KV cache exposure in the GLM-5.3 Workers AI model, and our remediation with SGLang**](https://blog.cloudflare.com/kv-cache-exposure-remediation) <span class='md-tag md-tag--warning'>[EN CONTENT]</span> 🌟🌟🌟🌟 <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[AI]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--secondary'>[CASE STUDY]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[EDGE]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--warning'>[EMERGING]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--success'>[ENTERPRISE-STABLE]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[GPU]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[LLM]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[SECURITY]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[SGLANG]</span> — Cloudflare engineers published a detailed remediation report on a cross-request KV cache exposure incident within the GLM-5.3 Workers AI model. Grounding confirms the root cause was a configuration mismatch in the open-source SGLang inference engine, which failed to properly isolate memory pages across requests. This post provides critical architectural insights into GPU memory pooling and the imperative of strict context length enforcement in multi-tenant LLM environments.

## Enterprise Architecture

### AI Integration

#### ERP Workflows

  - **(2026)** [**OpenAI expands partnership with Oracle to bring its apps to ChatGPT**](https://openai.com/index/oracle-apps-in-chatgpt) <span class='md-tag md-tag--warning'>[EN CONTENT]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--critical'>[ADVANCED LEVEL]</span> 🌟🌟🌟🌟 <span class='md-tag md-tag--success'>[DE FACTO STANDARD]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[ENTERPRISE-AI]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--success'>[ENTERPRISE-STABLE]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[LLM-INTEGRATION]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[ORACLE]</span> — OpenAI and Oracle have expanded their strategic partnership to integrate Oracle's enterprise applications and data natively into ChatGPT. The initiative introduces Oracle AI Workspace, allowing users to interact with secure enterprise workflows—such as financial analysis and resource planning—through an agentic interface. This integration represents a significant architectural shift towards unified, intent-driven administration secured by robust organizational governance.
### Productivity

#### Agentic Workflows (3)

  - **(2026)** [**ChatGPT Work and the Expanding World of Enterprise Automation**](https://openai.com/index/chatgpt-expanding-world-of-work) <span class='md-tag md-tag--warning'>[EN CONTENT]</span> 🌟🌟🌟🌟 <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[AI]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--warning'>[EMERGING]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--success'>[ENTERPRISE-STABLE]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[LLM]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[MCP]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[MLOPS]</span> — ChatGPT Work operates as an enterprise-grade agentic orchestration layer, leveraging advanced foundation models and the Model Context Protocol (MCP) to execute long-horizon automation across distributed operational workflows. While it accelerates developer velocity and simplifies Day-2 pipeline triggers, its proprietary SaaS architecture introduces third-party supply chain risks and mandates strict zero-trust RBAC and identity-aware proxies to mitigate prompt-driven privilege escalation. Although decoupled from internal Kubernetes control planes, it interfaces with cloud-native workloads via external API gateways, requiring disciplined ingress governance and telemetry to safely execute external agent actions.
### Supply Chain Optimization

#### Agentic Systems (4)

Furthermore, a post-trained 30-billion parameter Nemotron 3.5 Lightning model is introduced to codify human planner judgment into the automated decision workflow. While this proprietary orchestration platform accelerates physical hardware delivery ('wafer-out to first token'), Cloud Architects managing Kubernetes-based AI factories must balance the operational velocity gains against the vendor lock-in inherent to closed-ecosystem sovereign AI stacks.

## Hardware Infrastructure

### AI Accelerators

#### Power Management

  - **(2026)** [**How NVIDIA Groq 3 LPX Deterministic Execution Drives Power-Efficient High-Interactivity Inference on NVIDIA Vera Rubin**](https://developer.nvidia.com/blog/how-nvidia-groq-3-lpx-deterministic-execution-drives-power-efficient-high-interactivity-inference-on-nvidia-vera-rubin) <span class='md-tag md-tag--warning'>[EN CONTENT]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--critical'>[ADVANCED LEVEL]</span> 🌟🌟🌟🌟 <span class='md-tag md-tag--warning'>[EMERGING]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--success'>[ENTERPRISE-STABLE]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[GPU]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[HARDWARE]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[INFERENCE]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[POWER-EFFICIENCY]</span> — This engineering article examines the power-efficiency mechanisms of the Groq 3 LPX accelerator deployed within the NVIDIA Vera Rubin platform. By utilizing a deterministic execution model, the system accurately predicts electrical current draw, enabling Preemptive Power (PEP) and Clock Period Synthesis (CPS) techniques to shrink voltage guardbands safely. Coupled with NVIDIA DSX MaxLPS software, operators can recover stranded power and deploy up to 40% more GPUs within the same facility footprint. This architecture significantly advances the performance-per-watt metric critical for scaling modern AI factories and handling high-interactivity inference workloads.

### RTX Development

  - **(2026)** [**Code, Test, and Deploy on One Machine with NVIDIA RTX Spark**](https://developer.nvidia.com/blog/code-test-and-deploy-on-one-machine-with-nvidia-rtx-spark) <span class='md-tag md-tag--warning'>[EN CONTENT]</span> 🌟🌟🌟🌟 <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[AI-AGENTS]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[CUDA]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--warning'>[EMERGING]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--success'>[ENTERPRISE-STABLE]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[GPU]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[LLM]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[LOCAL-AI]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[WINDOWS-ON-ARM]</span> — NVIDIA RTX Spark combines the GB10 system-on-a-chip with up to 128GB of unified memory to empower developers to run local coding agents directly on their workstations. Specifically tuned to run models like Nemotron 3.5 Bolt 74B-A9B via engines like vLLM and llama.cpp, it drastically accelerates reasoning loops for single-machine deployment pipelines.

This provides AI researchers with a crucial empirical framework for refining benchmark purity and interpreting model evaluation data correctly.

## Model Optimization (3)

### GPU Kernels

  - **(2026)** [**Accelerating Mamba-2 with High-Performance Forward Pass Kernels in NVIDIA CUDA Tile**](https://developer.nvidia.com/blog/accelerating-mamba-2-with-high-performance-forward-pass-kernels-in-nvidia-cuda-tile) <span class='md-tag md-tag--warning'>[EN CONTENT]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--critical'>[ADVANCED LEVEL]</span> 🌟🌟🌟🌟 <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[CUDA]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--warning'>[EMERGING]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--success'>[ENTERPRISE-STABLE]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[GPU-PROGRAMMING]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[GPU]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[MAMBA]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[OPTIMIZATION]</span> — Engineers from Jane Street and NVIDIA have rewritten the forward pass of the Mamba-2 state-space model using NVIDIA CUDA Tile (cuTile), a Python-based DSL for tile-level GPU programming. By splitting sequences into chunks and utilizing matrix multiplications that leverage Tensor Cores, the custom kernels eliminate memory-bound bottlenecks typical in recurrent architectures. This chunkwise algorithmic optimization drastically accelerates training and prefill phases by abstracting thread- and warp-level scheduling behind high-level primitives.
## Multi-agent Systems

### Ad-tech

  - **(2026)** [**How we built ChatGPT's novel ad stack for user trust and relevance**](https://openai.com/index/building-chatgpt-ad-stack) <span class='md-tag md-tag--critical'>[ADVANCED LEVEL]</span> 🌟🌟🌟🌟 <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[AD-TECH]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[AGENTIC-WORKFLOW]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--warning'>[EMERGING]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--success'>[ENTERPRISE-STABLE]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[LLM-ORCHESTRATION]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[LLM]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[TOOL-CALLING]</span> — To support context-aware advertising within agentic chat, OpenAI architected a bespoke ad stack powered entirely by a specialized, post-trained LLM. Unlike traditional event-based ad systems, this orchestrator manages stateful ad sessions and interprets shifting user intent across multi-turn conversations. The model executes an agentic loop involving tool calls for retrieval, relevance filtering, and auction ranking, all while operating as an isolated service to prevent ad contamination of the core ChatGPT response. This rapid innovation allowed the platform to scale to a $1 billion ARR within 200 days of launch.
### Bioinformatics

  - **(2026)** [**Using thousands of AI agents to uncover new leads in biological data**](https://openai.com/index/scaling-ai-research-for-complex-biological-data) <span class='md-tag md-tag--critical'>[ADVANCED LEVEL]</span> 🌟🌟🌟🌟 <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[BIOINFORMATICS]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--secondary'>[CASE STUDY]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--warning'>[EMERGING]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--success'>[ENTERPRISE-STABLE]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[GPT-ROSALIND]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[MULTI-AGENT]</span> <span class='md-tag md-tag--primary'>[RESEARCH]</span> — In a massive display of distributed AI capabilities, OpenAI collaborated with PerturbAI and Scripps Research to unleash thousands of GPT-Rosalind agents on biological data. Analyzing the Whole Brain CRISPR Atlas, the agentic swarm mapped cellular responses to genetic perturbations across millions of mouse brain cells in just 32 minutes—a task that would normally take human researchers months. The system cross-referenced these findings with vast scientific literature, successfully generating novel, high-confidence hypotheses regarding neurodegenerative diseases like Alzheimer's and Parkinson's for human validation.
